## Part 20 — ALWAYS / NEVER print rules R1–R12 [AUD §5]

- **R1** A4 (Letter only on request), portrait (the 2-up exit slip landscape), printed at 100 %.
- **R2** Andika only (400 / 700), self-hosted or embedded, never Google Fonts, no italic.
- **R3** Three paints (#000, #FFF, #949494), plus the accent #5B2A86 on step marks and the key colour
  #C2410C on answers only.
- **R4** No emoji, pictographs or clip-art anywhere; line-art icons from Appendix B only.
- **R5** One I Can title for the whole pack, identical on every page.
- **R6** Strand tab + footer + © line on every page.
- **R7** Ruled cell grids with shared borders; no cards, no gutters, no dashed lines except the cut line.
- **R8** Slots from §8.10 at B(n), one width per section; never underscore characters.
- **R9** Facsimile keys for every pupil page with a slot; key ink only on answers.
- **R10** No composed writing on pupil pages; frames ≤ 2 blanks from a bank; no explain / prove.
- **R11** Every page ≥ 50 % used; no cell band ≥ 30 % empty (outside the answer / work zone); every item
  cell ≥ 40 % free.
- **R12** Content never shrinks; the size is L. Drop a row or add a page instead.

## Appendix A — House stylesheet (copy whole) [kit `sheet-kit.css` subset + lesson CSS + teacher classes]

Copy this block into a `<style>` element. Replace `FONT_URL` with the folder that holds
`Andika-Regular.woff2` and `Andika-Bold.woff2` (or replace each `url(...)` with a base64 data URL, §8.2).
Page markup pattern:

```html
<section class="pg pupil" data-page="P4a">                 <!-- one sheet; add class "key" for a key, "teacher" for T pages, "letter" for US Letter -->
  <div class="hd"><div class="rowA">
      <div class="fld name">Name <i></i></div><div class="fld date">Date <i></i></div>
      <div class="fld score">Score <i></i><b>/12</b></div>
      <div class="tab"><span>Level 1</span><span>Addition</span><span>Practice 1</span></div>
    </div><div class="title">I Can add 1–3 (facts to 10)</div><div class="hrule"></div></div>
  <div class="body">
    <div class="sstrip"><b>Steps:</b><ol><li><span class="num">1</span>[icon]<span>Start with the big number.</span></li>…</ol></div>
    <div class="instr">Add.</div>
    <div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);grid-template-rows:repeat(4,1fr)">
      <div class="cell" style="padding-top:8mm"><span class="lt">a.</span>[factStack(...)]</div> …
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="ft"><span>add_facts · Grade 1 · 1.OA.C.6 · EE …</span><span><b>1/1</b></span><span>Form A · seed 4242</span>
    <span class="copy">© 2026 Cultivating the Digital. All rights reserved.</span></div>
</section>
```
Lifted header (no fields): `<div class="hd"><div class="rowA"><div class="title">…</div><div class="tab">…</div></div><div class="hrule"></div></div>`.
A band: `<div class="band"><div class="strip"><b>Guided Practice:</b><span>Add.</span></div><div class="grid">…</div></div>`.
Answers carry `class="ka"` (HTML) or `data-ka` (SVG): hidden on pupil pages, key colour on keys.

```css v21-house
/* ===== V21 house stylesheet (copy whole; size L; A4 default) ===== */
/* 1. Font. Andika 6.x (SIL OFL 1.1), self-hosted. FONT_URL = folder that holds the two woff2 files. */
@font-face { font-family: "Andika"; font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; font-display: block;
  src: local("Andika"), local("Andika-Regular"), url("FONT_URL/Andika-Regular.woff2") format("woff2"); }
@font-face { font-family: "Andika"; font-style: normal; font-weight: 700; font-display: block;
  src: local("Andika Bold"), local("Andika-Bold"), url("FONT_URL/Andika-Bold.woff2") format("woff2"); }

/* 2. Tokens (size L). */
:root {
  --ink: #000; --paper: #fff; --grey: #949494; --accent: #5B2A86; --key: #C2410C;
  --rule: 2.25pt; --heavy: 1.5pt; --hair: .75pt; --fine: .5pt; --dot: 1pt;
  --digit: 28pt; --text: 15pt; --zone: 12pt; --letter: 10pt; --title: 18pt; --tabtext: 10pt; --frac: 20pt; --foot: 7pt;
  --hw: 10mm; --ans: 12mm; --instr: 9mm; --strip: 8mm; --slot-r: 1.5mm; --check: 7mm; --sq: 24mm;
  --t-body: 11pt; --t-say: 13pt; --t-sub: 13pt; --t-head: 15pt; --t-table: 10pt;
}

/* 3. Paper. One sheet = one .pg. A4 portrait default; .letter = US Letter; .land = A4 landscape (2-up only). */
@page { size: A4; margin: 0; }
@page letter { size: Letter; margin: 0; }
@page land { size: A4 landscape; margin: 0; }
html, body { margin: 0; padding: 0; background: #fff; }
*, *::before, *::after { box-sizing: border-box; }
.pg { width: 210mm; height: 297mm; padding: 12mm 12mm 14mm; display: flex; flex-direction: column; overflow: hidden;
  position: relative; break-after: page; page-break-after: always; background: var(--paper); color: var(--ink);
  font-family: "Andika", sans-serif; font-synthesis: none; font-style: normal; font-feature-settings: "cv04" 1;
  font-variant-numeric: lining-nums tabular-nums; font-size: var(--text); line-height: 1.3;
  -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; print-color-adjust: exact; }
.pg.letter { page: letter; width: 215.9mm; height: 279.4mm; padding: 12mm 14.95mm 14mm; }
.pg.land { page: land; width: 297mm; height: 210mm; padding: 12mm 13mm 12mm; }  /* live 271 x 186 mm (PG-5) */
.pg:last-child { break-after: auto; page-break-after: auto; }
@media screen { body { background: #fff; } .pg { margin: 0 auto 8mm; outline: 1px solid var(--grey); } }

/* 4. Header (HD). Full = 26 mm; lifted (no fields) = 17 mm; continuation = 12 mm. */
.hd { flex: none; }
.rowA { display: flex; align-items: flex-end; height: 14mm; gap: 5mm; position: relative; }
.fld { display: flex; align-items: flex-end; gap: 1.5mm; height: 11mm; font-size: var(--zone); font-weight: 700; white-space: nowrap; }
.fld i { display: block; height: 10mm; border-bottom: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); font-style: normal; font-weight: 700; font-size: var(--text); line-height: 10mm; padding-left: 2mm; }
.fld.name { flex: 1 1 auto; min-width: 55mm; max-width: 90mm; } .fld.name i { flex: 1; }
.fld.date i { width: 26mm; } .fld.score i { width: 14mm; }
.fld b { font-weight: 400; font-size: var(--text); }
.tab { flex: none; margin-left: auto; align-self: flex-start; width: 30mm; height: 14mm; border: var(--heavy) solid var(--ink);
  display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: center; text-align: center; font-size: var(--tabtext); line-height: 1.12; font-weight: 700; }
.tab span:last-child { font-weight: 400; }
.title { height: 8mm; margin-top: 1mm; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: var(--title); font-weight: 700; white-space: nowrap; }
.pg.land .title { white-space: normal; height: auto; min-height: 8mm; text-align: center; line-height: 1.15; }  /* a half-page title may wrap (HD-14) */
.rowA > .title { position: absolute; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; height: 14mm; margin: 0; pointer-events: none; } /* lifted title */
.hrule { border-top: var(--rule) solid var(--ink); margin: 1.2mm 0 1mm; }
.hd.cont .rowA { height: 8mm; }
.hd.cont .tab { height: 8mm; width: auto; padding: 0 3mm; flex-direction: row; align-items: center; font-size: 9pt; }

/* 5. Body, instruction line, footer, copyright. */
.body { flex: 1 1 0; min-height: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.instr { flex: none; height: var(--instr); display: flex; align-items: center; font-size: var(--text); }
.ft { flex: none; height: 6mm; margin-top: 3mm; display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr auto 1fr; align-items: end; column-gap: 4mm;
  font-size: var(--foot); line-height: 1; position: relative; }
.ft b { font-weight: 700; } .ft > span:nth-child(3) { text-align: right; }
.copy { position: absolute; left: 0; right: 0; top: 100%; margin-top: 1mm; text-align: center; font-size: 6.5pt; line-height: 1; }

/* 6. Bands (BD). Labels are bold, sentence case, end in a colon. */
.band { flex: none; display: flex; flex-direction: column; border: var(--heavy) solid var(--ink); border-top: var(--rule) solid var(--ink); margin-top: -1.5pt; }
.body > .band:first-child { margin-top: 1.5mm; border-top-width: var(--heavy); }
.teacher .body > .t > .band:first-child, .teacher .body > .band:first-child { margin-top: 3mm; }  /* no double rule under the header */
.band.grow { flex: 1 1 0; min-height: 0; }
.strip { flex: none; min-height: var(--strip); display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 3mm; padding: 0 3mm; font-size: var(--text); }
.strip b { font-weight: 700; }
.band > .grid, .band > .row { border-width: var(--heavy) 0 0 0; }
.band.sayb { border-top: var(--heavy) solid var(--ink); }
.sayline { flex: none; height: 13mm; display: flex; align-items: flex-end; gap: 3mm; padding: 0 3mm 2.5mm; font-size: var(--text); }
.sayline b { font-weight: 700; }

/* 7. Cell grid (CL). Gap-as-border: shared 0.75 pt lines never double. Rows are 1fr: equal cells. */
.grid { flex: 1 1 0; min-height: 0; display: grid; gap: var(--hair); background: var(--ink); border: var(--heavy) solid var(--ink); }
.grid.daily { gap: var(--heavy); }
.row { display: flex; }
.cell { position: relative; background: var(--paper); min-width: 0; min-height: 0; padding: 3mm; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; break-inside: avoid; page-break-inside: avoid; }
.cell.mid { justify-content: center; }
.lt { position: absolute; left: 1.5mm; top: 1mm; font-size: var(--letter); line-height: 1; }
.ntab { position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; min-width: 6mm; height: 6mm; padding: 0 .6mm; background: var(--ink); color: var(--paper);
  font-size: 11pt; font-weight: 700; line-height: 6mm; text-align: center; }
.ntab.w2 { min-width: 8.4mm; }

/* 8. Vertical fact stack (VA-70): 3 tracks of 0.72 em; sum rule 1.5 pt; answer row 12 mm. */
.stk { font-size: var(--digit); line-height: 1; display: grid; grid-template-columns: .95em .72em .72em; justify-content: center; }  /* operator track 0.95 em, digit tracks 0.72 em */
.stk > span { height: 1.15em; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; position: relative; }
.stk .op { font-weight: 700; }
.stk .rule { grid-column: 1 / -1; height: 0; border-top: var(--heavy) solid var(--ink); margin: 1mm -2.5mm 0; }  /* the sum rule overhangs the tracks by 2.5 mm each side */
.stk .ans { grid-column: 1 / -1; height: var(--ans); display: grid; grid-template-columns: .95em .72em .72em; }
.stk .ans > span { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; }
.ring { position: absolute; left: 50%; top: 50%; width: .82em; height: 1.08em; transform: translate(-50%, -50%); border: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); border-radius: 50%; }
.ring.g { border: var(--dot) solid var(--grey); }
.tile { position: absolute; left: 100%; top: 50%; transform: translateY(-50%); margin-left: 1.2mm; }

/* 9. Horizontal equations and slots (SL). */
.eq { font-size: var(--digit); line-height: 1.15; display: flex; align-items: flex-end; justify-content: center; gap: .28em; white-space: nowrap; }
.eq .o { font-weight: 700; width: .8em; text-align: center; }
.ln { display: inline-block; width: var(--w, 14mm); height: var(--hw); border-bottom: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); text-align: center; line-height: var(--hw); vertical-align: bottom; }
.bx { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; width: var(--w, 14mm); height: calc(var(--hw) + 2mm); border: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); border-radius: var(--slot-r); vertical-align: bottom; }
.sq { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; width: var(--sq); height: var(--sq); border: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); border-radius: var(--slot-r); font-size: var(--digit); flex: none; }
.circ { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; width: calc(var(--hw) + 2mm); height: calc(var(--hw) + 2mm); border: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); border-radius: 50%; vertical-align: bottom; }
.chk { display: inline-block; flex: none; width: var(--check); height: var(--check); border: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); border-radius: var(--slot-r); vertical-align: -1.6mm; margin-right: 2.5mm; position: relative; }
.unit { margin-left: 2mm; font-size: var(--text); }

/* Stacked fraction (TY-7): numerator over a 1.5 pt bar over denominator, 20 pt. */
.frac { display: inline-flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; font-size: var(--frac); line-height: 1.05; vertical-align: middle; margin: 0 .15em; }
.frac > span:first-child { border-bottom: var(--heavy) solid var(--ink); padding: 0 .2em .05em; }
.frac > span:last-child { padding: .05em .2em 0; }

/* 10. Text blocks: steps, story, vocabulary, chant. */
.num { flex: none; width: 6.4mm; height: 6.4mm; border: 1pt solid var(--accent); border-radius: 50%; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
  font-size: 12pt; font-weight: 700; color: var(--accent); line-height: 1; }
.num.big { width: 10mm; height: 10mm; border-width: 1.5pt; font-size: var(--title); }
.ico { flex: none; display: block; color: var(--accent); overflow: visible; }
.ico .f { fill: currentColor; stroke: none; }
.steps { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: space-evenly; gap: 2.2mm; font-size: 13pt; line-height: 1.2; }
.steps li { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 1.8mm; }
.sstrip { flex: none; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 3mm; padding: 1.6mm 0 2mm; border-bottom: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); font-size: 12pt; line-height: 1.2; }
.sstrip > b { font-size: var(--text); }
.sstrip ol { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: flex; gap: 5mm; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.sstrip li { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 1.4mm; }
.story { border: var(--heavy) solid var(--ink); border-radius: 3mm; padding: 3mm 4mm; line-height: 1.45; }
.story p { margin: 0; }
.chant { flex: 1; border: var(--heavy) solid var(--ink); border-radius: 3mm; padding: 1.2mm 5mm; font-size: var(--title); font-weight: 700; text-align: center; line-height: 1.25; }
.dotm { display: block; width: 2.5mm; height: 2.5mm; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--ink); }

/* 11. Cut line (2-up). Dashed = cut, nothing else. */
.cut { position: absolute; top: 8mm; bottom: 8mm; left: 50%; border-left: var(--hair) dashed var(--ink); }

/* 12. Answer key (AK, INK-31): key ink only on answer marks. */
.key .ka, .key [data-ka] { color: var(--key) !important; font-weight: 700; }
.key svg [data-ka] { stroke: var(--key); }
.key svg text[data-ka], .key svg .kf[data-ka] { fill: var(--key); stroke: none; }
.pupil .ka, .pupil [data-ka] { display: none; }
.teacher .ka { color: var(--key); font-weight: 700; }
.g, .tr { color: var(--grey); }

/* 13. Teacher pages (T-TYPE, T-SCRIPT, T-BOX, T-LOOP). */
.t { font-size: var(--t-body); line-height: 1.3; }
.t .strip { font-size: var(--t-head); }
.t h3 { font-size: var(--t-sub); font-weight: 700; margin: 0 0 1mm; }
.t p { margin: 0 0 1.5mm; }
.tpad { padding: 2mm 3mm; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2mm; }
.tline { font-size: var(--t-body); }
.tt { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: var(--t-table); line-height: 1.25; }
.tt th { text-align: left; font-weight: 700; border-bottom: var(--heavy) solid var(--ink); padding: 1mm 1.5mm; vertical-align: bottom; }
.tt td { border-bottom: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); padding: 1mm 1.5mm; vertical-align: top; }
.tt td.r, .tt th.r { text-align: right; }
.sc { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 6.4mm 14mm 1fr; column-gap: 2mm; row-gap: .8mm; padding: 1.6mm 0; border-bottom: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); align-items: baseline; }
.sc:last-child { border-bottom: 0; }
.sc > span.num { grid-column: 1; grid-row: 1 / span 6; align-self: start; }
.sc > b { font-weight: 700; font-size: var(--t-body); grid-column: 2; }
.sc > span { grid-column: 3; }
.sc .q { font-size: var(--t-say); }
.tbox { border: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); border-radius: 3mm; padding: 1.8mm 3mm; display: grid; grid-template-columns: 5.8mm 1fr; column-gap: 2.5mm; align-items: start; }
.tbox.watch { border-width: var(--heavy); }
.tbox svg { color: var(--ink); }
.loop { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 5mm 36mm 1fr; column-gap: 2mm; row-gap: 1mm; align-items: baseline; }
.cols2 { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; column-gap: 4mm; }
.thumb { width: 55mm; height: 77.8mm; flex: none; border: var(--hair) solid var(--ink); overflow: hidden; }
.thumb > .pg { transform: scale(.262); transform-origin: 0 0; margin: 0; outline: 0; break-after: auto !important; page-break-after: auto !important; }
.bline { display: flex; gap: 2mm; align-items: center; }
```

---

## Appendix B — Icon library [lesson-icons.js + V21 teacher icons, AUD T-ICON]

All icons are line art in a 10 × 10 box, drawn at 5.8 mm (8 mm on the anchor chart), stroke 1.5 pt
(converted to box units by the `icon()` function in Appendix D), round joins and caps, no fill except the
small marks with class `f`. Pupil step icons and teacher step icons are the accent purple; teacher box
icons are black. One distinct icon per step in a lesson; the same action always gets the same icon. The
path data is the `ICONS` object in Appendix D.

| id | Picture | Use |
|---|---|---|
| `look` | an eye | Look at / find |
| `start` | a flag on a start line | Start with / begin at |
| `count` | three dots under two hop arcs | Count on |
| `write` | a pencil | Write |
| `regroup` | a ten rod traded for ones | Regroup |
| `subtract` | a minus in a ring | Subtract / take away |
| `subOnes` / `subTens` | minus ring beside a cube / a rod | Subtract the ones / the tens |
| `ends` | a short number line with its end ticks marked | Find the two tens |
| `decide` | up and down arrows | Decide, round up or down |
| `check` | a check mark in a box | Check the work |
| `say` | a speech bubble | Say it |
| `read` | an open book | Word problem: Read it two times |
| `ringnum` | a numeral in a ring over a line | Word problem: Circle the numbers. Underline the question |
| `draw` | a squiggle in a frame | Word problem: Draw it |
| `tip` | a lightbulb | Teacher `Tip:` box |
| `language` | a speech bubble with "Aa" | Teacher ELL / language tip |
| `why` | a lightbulb with a line | "Why it works" tip |
| `feedback` | a speech bubble with a check mark | Teacher `Feedback:` box |
| `watch` | an eye with a mark | Teacher `Watch:` box |
| `research` | a book | Teacher `Research:` box |
| `key` | a key | Critical Atom |
| `cubes` | three cubes | Manipulative |
| `board` | a board on legs | Board |
| `mwb` | a small board | Mini whiteboard |
| `scissors` | scissors | The first cut line on a page |

## Appendix C — Instruction strings and oral frames [PED 10.1–10.5]

### C.1 The controlled instruction library

One string per task, reused word for word on every page. New strings may be added only by adding a key
here. The V21 addition is marked.

| Key | Instruction string | Used for |
|---|---|---|
| `add` | Add. | addition sections |
| `subtract` | Subtract. | subtraction sections |
| `multiply` | Multiply. | multiplication sections |
| `divide` | Divide. | division sections |
| `mixed-sign` | Add or subtract. Look at the _sign_. | mixed + and - |
| `mixed-ops` | Look at the _sign_. Solve. | any mixed operations |
| `missing` | Write the missing number. | unknowns |
| `missing-many` | Write the missing numbers. | several unknowns in one picture (a hundreds-chart window) |
| `trace-say` | Trace the answer. Say the steps. | Model cells |
| `say-write` | Say the fact. Then write the answer. | fact warm-ups |
| `rewrite-solve` | Write the problem in the grid. Then solve. | horizontal to vertical |
| `rewrite-only` | Write the problem in the grid. Do _not_ solve. | set-up only |
| `decide-regroup` | Do you need to regroup? Check one box. Do _not_ solve. | decide only |
| `notate-regroup` | Show the regrouping. Do _not_ subtract. | notate only |
| `circle-bigger` | Circle the bigger number. | cue set-up |
| `underline-first` | Underline the part you work first. | division, order of operations |
| `circle-sign` | Circle the sign. Then solve. | discrimination |
| `can-solve` | Circle the problems you can solve. Solve only those. | discrimination |
| `rule-yes-no` | Read the rule. Check one box: Yes or No. | rule pages (key renamed from `tick-rule` on 2026-09-19) |
| `compare` | Write <, > or = in the circle. | comparing |
| `order-up` | Write the numbers in order. Start with the smallest. | ordering |
| `count-write` | Count. Write the number. | counting |
| `draw-count` | Draw counters to show the number. | counting, ten frames |
| `ring-groups` | Circle groups of {n}. Write how many groups. | grouping, division |
| `groups-of` | Write how many groups. Write how many in each group. | equal groups |
| `array` | Write how many rows. Write how many in each row. | arrays |
| `skip-count` | Count by {n}. Write the missing numbers. | count-by strips |
| `expanded` | Write the number in expanded form. | place value |
| `digit-value` | Write the value of the underlined digit. | place value |
| `round` | Round to the nearest {place}. | rounding |
| `more-less` | Write 1 more and 1 less. | number sense (also 10, 100, 1,000) |
| `shade` | Shade the fraction. | fractions |
| `write-fraction` | Write the fraction. | fractions |
| `simplest-form` | Write the answer in simplest form. | the one step that asks for it (P-LG-15) |
| `denominator` | Count the equal parts. Write the denominator. | denominator only |
| `numerator` | Count the shaded parts. Write the numerator. | numerator only |
| `line-write` | Write the number at each dot. | number lines |
| `line-mark` | Mark the number on the line. | number lines |
| `time-write` | Write the time. | clocks |
| `time-draw` | Draw the hands. | clocks |
| `coins` | Count the coins. Write the total. | generic coins |
| `measure` | Measure the line. Write the length. | rulers |
| `convert` | Use the rule. Write the missing number. | conversions with a rule box |
| `graph` | Use the graph. Answer the questions. | data |
| `table` | Use the rule. Fill in the table. | in / out tables |
| `match` | Draw a line to match. | matching |
| `story` | Read the story. Fill in the diagram. Solve. | word problems v1 |
| `story-v2` | Solve. Write the number and the label. | word problems v2 |
| `story-k` | Show the story with lines. Write the equation. | K picture version |
| `check-fix` | Check the work. Check one box: Correct or Fix it. | error analysis |
| `check-by` | Multiply to check. Check one box: Correct or Fix it. | check-only steps |
| `judge-correct` | Correct | judge check box label (a label, not an instruction, so P-LG-1 does not apply); first of the two boxes of the judge frame; the wording is exactly this everywhere |
| `judge-not-correct` | Not correct | judge check box label; second of the two boxes, always paired with `judge-correct`; never "right / not right" or any other wording |
| `true-false` | Check one box: True or False. Finish the sentence. | True or False? |
| `spot` | Find the mistake. Circle it. Write the correct answer. | Reason It |
| `odd-one` | Circle the one that does not belong. Finish the sentence. | Reason It |
| `asn` | Check one box: Always, Sometimes or Never. Write an example. | Reason It |
| `which` | Which answer is correct? Circle A or B. | Reason It |
| `stretch` | Find more than one answer. Fill in the table. | Stretch |
| `cut-sort` | Cut. Sort. Glue. | hands-on sorts |
| `cut-order` | Cut. Put in order. Glue. | hands-on ordering |
| `find-color` | Find every {n}. Color it. | find-and-color |
| `line-jumps` | Draw the jumps on the line. Write the answer. | number-line addition and subtraction (added 2026-09-25) |
| `draw-blocks` | Draw tens and ones to show the number. | build a number with base-10 blocks (one vocabulary: tens and ones) |
| `draw-blocks-100` | Draw hundreds, tens and ones to show the number. | build a three-digit number with base-10 blocks |
| `check-groups` | Look at the groups. Check one box. | compare two groups: more, fewer, same |
| `how-many-left` | Write how many are left. | take away with crossed-out pictures |
| `count-all` | Count them all. Write how many. | join two pictured groups (add within 5 with pictures; added 2026-09-25) |
| `count-kind` | Count one kind. Write how many. | sort and count one kind in a mixed picture (added 2026-09-25) |
| `count-tens` | Write how many tens. | count rods or full ten frames as tens (added 2026-09-25) |
| `ring-remainder` | Circle groups of {n}. Write the quotient and the remainder. | division with a remainder, pictured (its two slots: `= [ ] R [ ]`) |
| `ring-groups-each` | Circle groups of the number shown. Write how many groups. | `ring-groups` when the section's items do not share one {n} (added 2026-09-25, critic round 2) |
| `ring-remainder-each` | Circle groups of the second number. Write the quotient and the remainder. | `ring-remainder` when the divisors differ (R3: "the divisor" was jargon on the page) |
| `missing-all` | Write the missing numbers. | `missing` when any item of the section has more than one blank |
| `story-k2` | Solve. Write the number. | Kindergarten word problems: the label word is printed |
| `story-work` | Circle the sign. Write the numbers in the boxes. Solve. | every whole-number word problem: the word-work cell (sign row, column boxes, answer and unit bank; owner ruling 2026-09-25) |
| `pick-parts` | Write one number from each list to make the sum. | find two addends from lists |
| `fact-family` | Use the three numbers. Fill in the fact family. | addition and subtraction fact families |
| `chart-fill` | Fill in the missing products. | multiplication chart |
| `groups-total` | Write the groups, the number in each, and the total. | arrays and equal groups |
| `check-clock` | Check the clock that shows the time. | time: find the clock for a digital time or for words (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `order-times` | Write 1, 2, 3 under the clocks. Start with the earliest. | time: order clocks, earliest first (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `order-times-late` | Write 1, 2, 3 under the clocks. Start with the latest. | time: order clocks, latest first (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `elapsed-end` | Use the time line. Write the end time. | elapsed time: find the end (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `elapsed-start` | Use the time line. Write the start time. | elapsed time: find the start (earlier) (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `elapsed-how-long` | Use the time line. Write how long it takes. | elapsed time: find the duration (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `hour-hand` | Which is the hour hand? Check one box. | parts of a clock: the hands (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `clock-numbers` | Write the missing numbers on the clock. | parts of a clock: the numerals (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `fives-ring` | Count by 5. Write the minutes round the clock. | the fives ring round a face (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `am-pm` | Read the time. Check a.m. or p.m. | a.m. or p.m. from an activity (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `money-count` | Count the money. Write the total. | count notes (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `money-two` | Count the notes, then the coins. Write both numbers. | count notes and coins, two unit numbers (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `money-add` | Add the prices. | add money in columns (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `money-change` | Subtract to find the change. | change in columns (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `coins-make` | Do the coins make the amount? Check one box. | does a coin set make an amount (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `fewest-coins` | Use the fewest coins. Write how many of each. | fewest coins, tally table (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `enough` | Is there enough money? Check one box. | enough money for a price (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `notes-order` | Write 1, 2, 3 under the notes. Start with the least. | order notes least to most (Qatari currency row) (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `coin-find` | Circle every coin worth the number. Write how many. | find the coins of one value (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `money-write` | Write the amount. Use the point. | write an amount with a decimal point (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `money-more` | Which has more money? Check one box. | compare two collections (P10, added 2026-09-25) |
| `table-rule` | Find the rule. Write the rule. | function table: every row given, the rule unknown (added 2026-09-25) |
| `table-in` | Use the rule backward. Write each In number. | function table: the In numbers missing |
| `table-make` | Write your own In numbers. Use the rule for Out. | function table: the pupil makes the rows |
| `count-by-row` | Count by the number in the box. Write the missing numbers. | count by 1-12: one row per table (added 2026-09-25) |
| `pattern-rule` | Use the rule. Write the missing numbers. | number patterns, the rule printed |
| `pattern-find-rule` | Find the rule. Write the missing numbers and the rule. | number patterns, the pupil writes the rule |
| `chart-fill-all` | Fill in the whole multiplication chart. | the blank chart (100%) |
| `chart-headers` | Fill in the missing row and column numbers. | multiplication chart: the factors on its edges |
| `shade-multiples` | Shade every multiple of {n}. | multiplication chart: find the multiples |
| `chart-row-rule` | Fill in the row. Write the rule. | multiplication chart: a row's pattern |
| `hop-draw` | Draw the hops on the line. Write the product. | multiplication on a number line |
| `hop-draw-div` | Draw hops of the number you divide by. Write how many hops. | division on a number line |
| `hop-sentence` | Look at the hops. Write the number sentence. | × / ÷ on a number line, hops drawn |
| `hop-missing` | Look at the hops. Write the missing number. | × / ÷ on a number line, one number missing |
| `frac-name` | Write the fraction, or circle the model that shows it. | identify fractions: write the fraction a model shows, or pick the model (A-D) that shows a fraction (added 2026-09-25, O6 lane AP3) |
| `models-complete` | Look at the two models. Complete the number sentence. | equivalent fractions with two models: the missing fraction, number, or = / ≠ (added 2026-09-25, O6 lane AP3) |
| `line-mark-each` | Mark each number on the line. | put fractions, mixed numbers, decimals or integers on a number line: a dot on the tick, and the number's letter when there are several (added 2026-09-25, O6 lane AP3) |
| `read-thermometer` | Read the thermometer. Write the temperature. | read a thermometer: one box, the unit printed after it (added 2026-09-25, O6 lane AP2) |
| `read-ruler` | Read the ruler. Write the number the arrow points to. | read an inch ruler at true scale: one answer slot, a whole box and a stacked fraction on a half / quarter ruler (added 2026-09-25, O6 lane AP2) |
| `add-sides` | Add the lengths of all the sides. Write the perimeter. | perimeter of a drawn rectangle, square or triangle (added 2026-09-25, O6 lane AP2) |
| `tally` | Use the tally chart. Answer the questions. | read a tally chart: one box, or one check box for the row with the most / least (added 2026-09-25, O6 lane AP2) |
| `default-write` | Solve. Write the answer. | default adapter: skills with no provider, number or text answer |
| `default-circle` | Circle the answer. | default adapter: choice items |
| `default-circle-all` | Circle all the correct answers. | default adapter: multi-select items |
| `default-order` | Write the numbers in order. | default adapter: ordering items with no stated direction |
| `default-solve` | Solve. | default adapter: any other answer type |
| `story-rcdws` (V21) | Read the story. Draw it. Solve. | word problem v1 with the RCDWS panel (P5) |
| `partition` (V21) | Draw a line to make 2 equal parts. | partition a shape into halves (Appendix H) |
| `equal-parts` (V21) | Are the parts equal? Check one box. | judge equal parts; labels `Equal parts` / `Not equal parts` |

Rules: at most 12 words and three sentences, each starting with a print verb (a question may open a
decide instruction); the same task always gets the same string; "Do _not_ solve." always last, with
_not_ underlined; never explain, describe, justify, discuss, prove or "in your own words"; one
instruction line per section, never repeated inside cells; `Check` names the box ("Check one box.") or
the work ("Check the work."), never an option word.

### C.2 Print verbs

`Look` `Read` `Say` `Count` `Circle` `Underline` `Box` `Cross out` `Trace` `Write` `Draw` `Shade`
`Mark` `Match` `Measure` `Use` `Find` `Fill in` `Finish` `Check` `Fix` `Think` `Start` `Put` `Move`
`Add` `Subtract` `Multiply` `Divide` `Solve` `Round` `Compare` `Estimate` `Regroup` `Bring down`
`Cut` `Sort` `Glue` `Color`. Never on paper: tap, click, type, drag, select, enter, press, swipe, scroll.

### C.3 Oral frames (the `Say:` band and teacher scripts)

| Context | Oral frame |
|---|---|
| Addition | “__ plus __ equals __.” |
| Subtraction | “__ minus __ equals __.” |
| Multiplication | “__ groups of __ is __.” then “__ times __ equals __.” |
| Division | “__ divided by __ equals __.” / “… equals __, remainder __.” |
| Regroup decision | “__ is less than __. I need to regroup.” / “I do not need to regroup.” |
| Place value | “__ hundreds, __ tens, __ ones.” |
| Compare | “__ is greater than __.” / “__ is less than __.” / “__ is equal to __.” |
| Fractions | “__ out of __ equal parts are shaded.” |
| Rounding | “__ is between __ and __. It is closer to __.” |
| Time | “The time is __.” |
| Measurement | “The line is __ {unit} long.” |
| Word problem | “The answer is __ {unit}.” |
| Error analysis (oral only) | “The mistake is in the __. The correct answer is __.” |

A frame is one or two sentences of ≤ 12 words each; blanks only for numbers, unit words or a bank word.
On a printed `Say:` band the frame carries at most 2 blanks; on the anchor chart it is filled in.

### C.4 "Remember … NOT …" contrast lines (one per lesson at most, in `Remember:` or `Rule:`)

| Confusion | Line |
|---|---|
| tenths / tens | Remember: tenths are parts of one, NOT groups of ten. |
| denominator | Remember: the denominator counts all the equal parts, NOT only the shaded parts. |
| number-line fractions | Remember: count the spaces, NOT the marks. |
| perimeter / area | Remember: perimeter goes around the shape, NOT inside it. |
| < and > | Remember: the small end points to the smaller number, NOT the bigger one. |
| regrouping | Remember: the ten you regroup is still there. Add it, do NOT forget it. |
| unit fractions | Remember: more parts means smaller parts, NOT bigger parts. |
| "fewer than" | Remember: "fewer than" compares two amounts. It does NOT always mean subtract. |
| minute hand | Remember: the long hand shows minutes. Count by 5, NOT by 1. |
| zero in a number | Remember: the zero holds a place. Do NOT leave it out. |

---

## Appendix D — Drawing recipes (copy whole) [WDS 10–11, minimums at L]

Pure functions that return SVG or HTML strings. Every drawing is in millimetres at size L, Andika text,
strokes from §8.5, grey only for shaded parts and hints, answers tagged `data-ka` / `class="ka"` so they
show only on keys. Minimum sizes are in §19.3. Each function's comment states its drawing rules.

```js v21-recipes
// ===== V21 drawing recipes (copy whole). Pure functions -> SVG / HTML strings. =====
// Units: every viewBox is in millimetres and the <svg> width/height are the same numbers in mm,
// so 1 viewBox unit = 1 mm on paper. Strokes are given in pt and converted (1 pt = 0.3528 mm).
// Text is Andika. Ink: '#000'; the one grey '#949494'; nothing else on a pupil drawing.
// `ka` marks an answer mark: it is drawn only on the key page, in the key colour (CSS .key [data-ka]).
const PT = 0.3528;                       // mm per pt
const S = { rule: 2.25 * PT, heavy: 1.5 * PT, hair: 0.75 * PT, fine: 0.5 * PT, dot: 1 * PT };
const FS = { zone: 12 * PT, digit: 28 * PT, text: 15 * PT };   // font sizes in mm (4.23 / 9.88 / 5.29)
const GREY = '#949494';
const r2 = (n) => Math.round(n * 100) / 100;
const svg = (w, h, body, cls = '') =>
  `<svg class="${cls}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 ${r2(w)} ${r2(h)}" width="${r2(w)}mm" height="${r2(h)}mm" ` +
  `style="display:block;overflow:visible;font-family:Andika,sans-serif" fill="none" stroke="#000" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">${body}</svg>`;
const txt = (x, y, s, size = FS.zone, o = {}) =>
  `<text x="${r2(x)}" y="${r2(y)}" font-size="${r2(size)}" text-anchor="${o.anchor || 'middle'}" font-weight="${o.bold ? 700 : 400}" ` +
  `fill="${o.fill || '#000'}" stroke="none"${o.ka ? ' data-ka' : ''}>${s}</text>`;
const arrowHead = (x, y, ang, fill = '#000', ka = false) => {      // LS-7: solid head 2.5 x 2 mm
  const c = Math.cos(ang), s = Math.sin(ang), L = 2.5, W = 1;
  const p = [[x, y], [x - L * c - W * s, y - L * s + W * c], [x - L * c + W * s, y - L * s - W * c]];
  return `<path d="M${p.map((q) => q.map(r2).join(' ')).join(' L')} Z" fill="${fill}" stroke="none"${ka ? ' data-ka class="kf"' : ''}/>`;
};

// RP-50..52. Number line. Minimum at L: tick pitch 8 mm, length >= 160 mm (WDS 11.2).
// hops: [{from, to, mode:'given'|'model'|'answer'}] -> arcs above the line (given solid, model dotted grey).
export function numberLine({ from = 0, to = 10, pitch = 16, labels = 'all', hide = [], hops = [], arrows = 'right', labelSize = FS.zone, marks = [] } = {}) {
  const n = to - from, pad = 6, len = n * pitch, w = len + pad * 2, y = 18, h = 28;
  let b = `<path d="M${pad - 3} ${y} H${r2(pad + len + 3)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`;
  if (arrows !== 'none') b += arrowHead(pad + len + 4.5, y, 0);
  if (arrows === 'both') b += arrowHead(pad - 4.5, y, Math.PI);
  for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
    const x = pad + i * pitch, v = from + i;
    b += `<path d="M${r2(x)} ${y - 2.5} V${y + 2.5}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`;
    if ((labels === 'all' || (Array.isArray(labels) && labels.includes(v))) && !hide.includes(v)) b += txt(x, y + 8, v, labelSize);
  }
  for (const m of marks) b += `<circle cx="${r2(pad + (m.v - from) * pitch)}" cy="${y}" r="1.25" fill="#000" stroke="none"${m.ka ? ' data-ka class="kf"' : ''}/>`;
  for (const hp of hops) {
    const x1 = pad + (hp.from - from) * pitch, x2 = pad + (hp.to - from) * pitch, mx = (x1 + x2) / 2, top = y - 12;
    const grey = hp.mode === 'model', ka = hp.mode === 'answer';
    b += `<path d="M${r2(x1)} ${y - 1} Q${r2(mx)} ${top} ${r2(x2 - 0.6)} ${y - 2.2}" stroke-width="${r2(grey ? S.dot : S.hair)}"` +
         `${grey ? ` stroke="${GREY}" stroke-dasharray="0 ${r2(1.2)}"` : ''}${ka ? ' data-ka' : ''}/>`;
    b += arrowHead(x2 - 0.2, y - 1.6, Math.PI * 0.38, grey ? GREY : '#000', ka);
  }
  return svg(w, h, b, 'v-nl');
}

// Count-on hop track (the add 1-3 picture): numbers in a row, hop arcs between them.
// state per number: 'ink' | 'grey' ; arcs: 'ink' | 'grey'.
export function hopTrack({ nums = [7, 8, 9], numState = [], arcState = [], pitch = 11, size = FS.text } = {}) {
  const w = (nums.length - 1) * pitch + 10, h = 15, y = 13.5;
  let b = '';
  nums.forEach((v, i) => { b += txt(5 + i * pitch, y, v, size, { bold: true, fill: numState[i] === 'grey' ? GREY : '#000' }); });
  for (let i = 0; i < nums.length - 1; i++) {
    const x1 = 5 + i * pitch + 0.8, x2 = 5 + (i + 1) * pitch - 0.8, col = arcState[i] === 'grey' ? GREY : '#000';
    b += `<path d="M${r2(x1)} 6.6 Q${r2((x1 + x2) / 2)} 0.6 ${r2(x2)} 5.6" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`;
    b += arrowHead(x2 + 0.15, 6.3, Math.PI * 0.4, col);
  }
  return svg(w, h, b, 'v-hop');
}

// RP-54. Number track: one rounded outline (3 mm, 1.5 pt), 0.75 pt dividers, boxes 18 x 16 mm, numerals 28 pt.
// vals: numbers, or null for the missing entry, which is drawn as a writing box (B(1) = 14 mm). ans = key value.
export function numberTrack(vals, ans = '', { w = 18, h = 16 } = {}) {
  return `<div style="display:flex;border:1.5pt solid #000;border-radius:3mm;overflow:hidden" data-slot="track">` +
    vals.map((v, i) => `<span style="width:${w}mm;height:${h}mm;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:28pt;${i ? 'border-left:.75pt solid #000;' : ''}">` +
      (v == null ? `<span class="bx" data-slot="box" style="--w:14mm"><b class="ka">${ans}</b></span>` : v) + `</span>`).join('') + `</div>`;
}

// RP-10/11. Ten frame 2 x 5, cell 10 mm at L (pupil draws: 11 mm). a = solid counters, b = hollow, dotted = to trace.
export function tenFrame({ a = 0, b = 0, cell = 10, vertical = false, dotted = 0 } = {}) {
  const cols = vertical ? 2 : 5, rows = vertical ? 5 : 2, w = cols * cell, h = rows * cell;
  let s = `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`;
  for (let c = 1; c < cols; c++) s += `<path d="M${c * cell} 0 V${h}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`;
  for (let r = 1; r < rows; r++) s += `<path d="M0 ${r * cell} H${w}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`;
  const rad = cell * 0.65 / 2;
  for (let i = 0; i < a + b + dotted && i < 10; i++) {
    const c = vertical ? i % 2 : i % 5, r = vertical ? Math.floor(i / 2) : Math.floor(i / 5);
    const cx = c * cell + cell / 2, cy = r * cell + cell / 2;
    if (i < a) s += `<circle cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" r="${r2(rad)}" fill="#000" stroke="none"/>`;
    else if (i < a + b) s += `<circle cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" r="${r2(rad)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`;
    else s += `<circle cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" r="${r2(rad)}" stroke="${GREY}" stroke-width="${r2(S.dot)}" stroke-dasharray="0 ${r2(1.2)}"/>`;
  }
  return svg(w, h, s, 'v-tf');
}

// RP-20/21. Counters or in-house pictures to count: rows of at most 5, pitch = item + 3 mm, item 12 mm at L.
const PICS = {
  dot: (x, y, d) => `<circle cx="${r2(x + d / 2)}" cy="${r2(y + d / 2)}" r="${r2(d * 0.42)}" fill="#000" stroke="none"/>`,
  ring: (x, y, d) => `<circle cx="${r2(x + d / 2)}" cy="${r2(y + d / 2)}" r="${r2(d * 0.42)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`,
  triangle: (x, y, d) => `<path d="M${r2(x + d / 2)} ${r2(y + 0.6)} L${r2(x + d - 0.6)} ${r2(y + d - 0.8)} L${r2(x + 0.6)} ${r2(y + d - 0.8)} Z" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`,
  square: (x, y, d) => `<rect x="${r2(x + 1)}" y="${r2(y + 1)}" width="${r2(d - 2)}" height="${r2(d - 2)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`,
  star: (x, y, d) => { const cx = x + d / 2, cy = y + d / 2 + 0.4, R = d * 0.48, r = R * 0.45; let p = '';
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { const a = -Math.PI / 2 + i * Math.PI / 5, q = i % 2 ? r : R; p += `${i ? 'L' : 'M'}${r2(cx + q * Math.cos(a))} ${r2(cy + q * Math.sin(a))} `; }
    return `<path d="${p}Z" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`; },
};
export function counters({ n = 5, kind = 'dot', item = 12, perRow = 5 } = {}) {
  const pitch = item + 3, rows = Math.ceil(n / perRow), cols = Math.min(n, perRow);
  const w = cols * pitch - 3, h = rows * pitch - 3;
  let s = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s += PICS[kind]((i % perRow) * pitch, Math.floor(i / perRow) * pitch, item);
  return svg(w, h, s, 'v-cnt');
}

// Dot tile (a die face for 1-3): rounded 1 mm (object under 8 mm side uses 1 mm), side 9 mm at L.
export function dotTile({ n = 2, side = 9, grey = false } = {}) {
  const c = grey ? GREY : '#000', sw = grey ? S.dot : S.hair, d = 1.0;
  const P = { 1: [[.5, .5]], 2: [[.27, .27], [.73, .73]], 3: [[.25, .25], [.5, .5], [.75, .75]] }[n] || [];
  let s = `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${side}" height="${side}" rx="1.2" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${r2(sw)}"/>`;
  for (const [px, py] of P) s += `<circle cx="${r2(px * side)}" cy="${r2(py * side)}" r="${d}" fill="${c}" stroke="none"/>`;
  return svg(side, side, s, 'v-tile');
}

// RP-30. Base-ten blocks, gridded. u = 3.5 mm at L (up to 99). Rods vertical (u x 10u), units 2 wide x 5 high.
export function base10({ hundreds = 0, tens = 0, ones = 0, u = 3.5 } = {}) {
  let s = '', x = 0; const H = 10 * u;
  for (let i = 0; i < hundreds; i++, x += 10 * u + 2) {
    s += `<rect x="${r2(x)}" y="0" width="${r2(10 * u)}" height="${r2(H)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`;
    for (let k = 1; k < 10; k++) s += `<path d="M${r2(x + k * u)} 0 V${r2(H)} M${r2(x)} ${r2(k * u)} H${r2(x + 10 * u)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.fine)}"/>`;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < tens; i++, x += u + 1) {
    s += `<rect x="${r2(x)}" y="0" width="${r2(u)}" height="${r2(H)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`;
    for (let k = 1; k < 10; k++) s += `<path d="M${r2(x)} ${r2(k * u)} H${r2(x + u)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.fine)}"/>`;
  }
  if (ones) x += 2;
  for (let i = 0; i < ones; i++) {
    const c = i % 2, r = Math.floor(i / 2);
    s += `<rect x="${r2(x + c * (u + 1))}" y="${r2(H - (r + 1) * (u + 1) + 1)}" width="${r2(u)}" height="${r2(u)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`;
  }
  const w = x + (ones ? 2 * (u + 1) : 0);
  return svg(Math.max(w, 1), H, s, 'v-b10');
}

// RP-31. Quick-draw: open square = hundred (8 mm), stick = ten (10 mm), open dot = one (2.4 mm). All 1.5 pt.
// crossTens / crossOnes: how many of the drawn tens / ones are crossed out (a 0.75 pt diagonal: taken away).
export function quickDraw({ hundreds = 0, tens = 0, ones = 0, crossTens = 0, crossOnes = 0 } = {}) {
  let s = '', x = 1; const sw = r2(S.heavy), xw = r2(S.hair);
  for (let i = 0; i < hundreds; i++, x += 10) s += `<rect x="${x}" y="1" width="8" height="8" stroke-width="${sw}"/>`;
  if (hundreds) x += 2;
  for (let i = 0; i < tens; i++, x += 3.5) { s += `<path d="M${r2(x + 1)} 0.5 V10.5" stroke-width="${sw}"/>`;
    if (i >= tens - crossTens) s += `<path d="M${r2(x - 0.4)} 7.5 L${r2(x + 2.4)} 3.5" stroke-width="${xw}"/>`; }
  if (tens) x += 2;
  for (let i = 0; i < ones; i++) { const cx = x + 1.5 + (i % 5) * 3.6, cy = 3 + Math.floor(i / 5) * 5;
    s += `<circle cx="${r2(cx)}" cy="${cy}" r="1.2" stroke-width="${sw}"/>`;
    if (i >= ones - crossOnes) s += `<path d="M${r2(cx - 1.6)} ${cy + 1.6} L${r2(cx + 1.6)} ${cy - 1.6}" stroke-width="${xw}"/>`; }
  return svg(x + Math.min(ones, 5) * 3.6 + 2, Math.max(11, 6 + 5 * Math.ceil(ones / 5)), s, 'v-qd');
}

// RP-40. Place-value chart: 1.5 pt outline, 0.75 pt interior; column 17 mm x row 12 mm at L; heads in words.
export function pvChart({ heads = ['Tens', 'Ones'], rows = [['', '']], col = 17, row = 12, headH = 8 } = {}) {
  const w = heads.length * col, h = headH + rows.length * row;
  let s = `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`;
  s += `<path d="M0 ${headH} H${w}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`;
  heads.forEach((t, i) => { if (i) s += `<path d="M${i * col} 0 V${h}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`; s += txt(i * col + col / 2, headH - 2.2, t, FS.zone, { bold: true }); });
  rows.forEach((r, j) => { if (j) s += `<path d="M0 ${headH + j * row} H${w}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`;
    r.forEach((v, i) => { if (v !== '' && v != null) s += txt(i * col + col / 2, headH + j * row + row / 2 + 3.4, typeof v === 'object' ? v.v : v, FS.digit * 0.85, { ka: typeof v === 'object' && v.ka }); }); });
  const wide = Math.max(w, heads.length * 14);
  return svg(wide, h, s, 'v-pv');
}

// RP-70/80. Part-whole bar model. Bars 14 mm tall at L, min length 42 mm. Never proportional to the numbers (RP-72):
// parts are always equal length. The unknown is a solid box with '?' in its top-left (zone size). Values in mm text at digit size.
export function barModel({ whole = '?', parts = ['?', '?'], len = 120, h = 14, gap = 4 } = {}) {
  const W = len, ph = h, y2 = h + gap, pw = W / parts.length;
  const cellTxt = (x, y, wd, v) => v === '?' ? txt(x + 1.6, y + 4.6, '?', FS.zone, { anchor: 'start', bold: true })
    : (v && typeof v === 'object') ? txt(x + wd / 2, y + h / 2 + 3.3, v.v, FS.digit, { ka: true }) : txt(x + wd / 2, y + h / 2 + 3.3, v, FS.digit);
  let s = `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${W}" height="${h}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>` + cellTxt(0, 0, W, whole);
  parts.forEach((v, i) => { s += `<rect x="${r2(i * pw)}" y="${y2}" width="${r2(pw)}" height="${ph}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>` + cellTxt(i * pw, y2, pw, v); });
  return svg(W, y2 + ph, s, 'v-bar');
}

// RP-81. Array: open circles 0.6 x pitch, 0.75 pt; pitch 9 mm at L (up to 5 x 5); 1.5 mm gap after the 5th row/col.
export function arrayDots({ rows = 3, cols = 4, pitch = 9, solid = false } = {}) {
  const g = (k) => (k >= 5 ? 1.5 : 0), w = cols * pitch + g(cols - 1), h = rows * pitch + g(rows - 1);
  let s = '';
  for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++)
    s += `<circle cx="${r2(c * pitch + pitch / 2 + g(c))}" cy="${r2(r * pitch + pitch / 2 + g(r))}" r="${r2(pitch * 0.3)}"` +
         (solid ? ' fill="#000" stroke="none"/>' : ` stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`);
  return svg(w, h, s, 'v-arr');
}

// RP-82. Equal groups: rounded outlines 30 x 22 mm at L, same arrangement in every group (solid dots 4.5 mm).
export function equalGroups({ groups = 3, each = 4, ow = 30, oh = 22, gap = 4 } = {}) {
  let s = '';
  for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
    const x0 = g * (ow + gap);
    s += `<rect x="${x0}" y="0" width="${ow}" height="${oh}" rx="6" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`;
    const perRow = Math.min(each, 3), rows = Math.ceil(each / perRow);
    for (let i = 0; i < each; i++) { const c = i % perRow, r = Math.floor(i / perRow);
      s += `<circle cx="${r2(x0 + ow / 2 + (c - (perRow - 1) / 2) * 7)}" cy="${r2(oh / 2 + (r - (rows - 1) / 2) * 7)}" r="2.25" fill="#000" stroke="none"/>`; }
  }
  return svg(groups * (ow + gap) - gap, oh, s, 'v-eg');
}

// RP-60. Number bond: whole box above (1.5 pt), part boxes below (0.75 pt), side 20 mm at L, joined by 0.75 pt lines.
// A value null = an empty writing box; {v, ka:true} = an answer drawn only on the key.
export function numberBond({ whole = null, parts = [null, null], side = 20, spread = 64, drop = 22 } = {}) {
  const W = spread + side, cx = W / 2, yP = side + drop;
  const box = (x, y, v, heavy) => `<rect x="${r2(x)}" y="${r2(y)}" width="${side}" height="${side}" rx="1.5" stroke-width="${r2(heavy ? S.heavy : S.hair)}"/>` +
    (v == null ? '' : txt(x + side / 2, y + side / 2 + 3.4, typeof v === 'object' ? v.v : v, FS.digit, { bold: true, ka: typeof v === 'object' && v.ka }));
  let s = `<path d="M${r2(cx)} ${side} L${r2(side / 2)} ${r2(yP)} M${r2(cx)} ${side} L${r2(W - side / 2)} ${r2(yP)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`;
  s += box(cx - side / 2, 0, whole, true) + box(0, yP, parts[0], false) + box(W - side, yP, parts[1], false);
  return svg(W, yP + side, s, 'v-bond');
}

// RP-100/101. Analog clock, D = 50 mm at L (hour / half hour). hands:false = draw-the-hands face.
export function clock({ h = 3, m = 0, D = 50, hands = true } = {}) {
  const R = D / 2, c = R; let s = `<circle cx="${c}" cy="${c}" r="${R}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`;
  s += `<circle cx="${c}" cy="${c}" r="${r2(0.9 * R)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.fine)}"/>`;
  for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const a = i * Math.PI / 30, five = i % 5 === 0, r1 = 0.9 * R, r2_ = (five ? 0.8 : 0.85) * R;
    s += `<path d="M${r2(c + r1 * Math.sin(a))} ${r2(c - r1 * Math.cos(a))} L${r2(c + r2_ * Math.sin(a))} ${r2(c - r2_ * Math.cos(a))}" stroke-width="${r2(five ? S.hair : S.fine)}"/>`; }
  const fs = Math.max(0.12 * D, FS.zone);
  for (let k = 1; k <= 12; k++) { const a = k * Math.PI / 6; s += txt(c + 0.66 * R * Math.sin(a), c - 0.66 * R * Math.cos(a) + fs * 0.35, k, fs, { bold: true }); }
  if (hands) {
    const ha = (30 * (h % 12) + 0.5 * m) * Math.PI / 180, ma = 6 * m * Math.PI / 180;
    s += `<path d="M${c} ${c} L${r2(c + 0.46 * R * Math.sin(ha))} ${r2(c - 0.46 * R * Math.cos(ha))}" stroke-width="${r2(S.rule)}"/>`;
    s += `<path d="M${c} ${c} L${r2(c + 0.78 * R * Math.sin(ma))} ${r2(c - 0.78 * R * Math.cos(ma))}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`;
  }
  s += `<circle cx="${c}" cy="${c}" r="1" fill="#000" stroke="none"/>`;
  return svg(D, D, s, 'v-clock');
}

// RP-90. Fraction bar 56 x 18 mm at L: outline 1.5 pt, dividers 0.75 pt, shading flat grey from the left.
export function fracBar({ den = 4, shaded = 1, w = 56, h = 18, partition = true } = {}) {
  let s = ''; const pw = w / den;
  for (let i = 0; i < shaded; i++) s += `<rect x="${r2(i * pw)}" y="0" width="${r2(pw)}" height="${h}" fill="${GREY}" stroke="none"/>`;
  if (partition) for (let i = 1; i < den; i++) s += `<path d="M${r2(i * pw)} 0 V${h}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`;
  s += `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`;
  return svg(w, h, s, 'v-fbar');
}

// RP-90. Fraction circle D = 50 mm at L: sectors from 12 o'clock clockwise, outline 1.5 pt, partitions 0.75 pt.
export function fracCircle({ den = 4, shaded = 1, D = 50 } = {}) {
  const R = D / 2; let s = '';
  const pt = (k) => [R + R * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * k / den), R - R * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * k / den)];
  for (let i = 0; i < shaded; i++) { const [x1, y1] = pt(i), [x2, y2] = pt(i + 1);
    s += `<path d="M${R} ${R} L${r2(x1)} ${r2(y1)} A${R} ${R} 0 ${den === 1 ? 1 : 0} 1 ${r2(x2)} ${r2(y2)} Z" fill="${GREY}" stroke="none"/>`; }
  for (let i = 0; i < den && den > 1; i++) { const [x, y] = pt(i); s += `<path d="M${R} ${R} L${r2(x)} ${r2(y)}" stroke-width="${r2(S.hair)}"/>`; }
  s += `<circle cx="${R}" cy="${R}" r="${R}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`;
  return svg(D, D, s, 'v-fcirc');
}

// RP-94. Hundredths grid: side 45 mm at L, outline 1.5 pt, interior 0.5 pt, 0.75 pt every 5; shaded cells grey, row by row.
export function hundredths({ shaded = 0, side = 45 } = {}) {
  const c = side / 10; let s = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < shaded; i++) s += `<rect x="${r2((i % 10) * c)}" y="${r2(Math.floor(i / 10) * c)}" width="${r2(c)}" height="${r2(c)}" fill="${GREY}" stroke="none"/>`;
  for (let k = 1; k < 10; k++) { const sw = r2(k === 5 ? S.hair : S.fine);
    s += `<path d="M${r2(k * c)} 0 V${side} M0 ${r2(k * c)} H${side}" stroke-width="${sw}"/>`; }
  s += `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${side}" height="${side}" stroke-width="${r2(S.heavy)}"/>`;
  return svg(side, side, s, 'v-hgrid');
}

// Step icons (lesson accent, 1.5 pt line art in a 10 x 10 box drawn at 5.8 mm at L). `.f` = solid mark.
export const ICONS = {
  look: '<path d="M0.8 5 Q5 0.6 9.2 5 Q5 9.4 0.8 5 Z"/><circle cx="5" cy="5" r="1.9"/><circle cx="5" cy="5" r="0.9" class="f"/>',
  start: '<path d="M2.2 9.6 V0.8"/><path d="M2.2 1.2 L8.8 3.3 L2.2 5.4 Z"/><path d="M0.8 9.6 H4.6"/>',
  count: '<circle cx="1.8" cy="7.6" r="0.9" class="f"/><circle cx="5" cy="7.6" r="0.9" class="f"/><circle cx="8.2" cy="7.6" r="0.9" class="f"/><path d="M1.8 5.2 Q3.4 1.6 5 5.2 Q6.6 1.6 8.2 5.2"/><path d="M7 4.3 L8.2 5.3 L8.6 3.8"/>',
  write: '<path d="M2.4 9.4 L1 9.9 L1.5 8.5 Z" class="f"/><path d="M1.5 8.5 L7.4 2.6 L8.8 4 L2.9 9.9 Z"/><path d="M7.4 2.6 L8.4 1.6 Q9 1 9.6 1.6 Q10 2.2 9.8 2.6 L8.8 4"/>',
  regroup: '<rect x="0.8" y="0.8" width="2" height="8.4"/><path d="M0.8 2.5 H2.8 M0.8 4.2 H2.8 M0.8 5.9 H2.8 M0.8 7.6 H2.8"/><path d="M3.8 5 H6"/><path d="M5.2 4.1 L6.2 5 L5.2 5.9"/><rect x="6.8" y="2.6" width="1.3" height="1.3"/><rect x="8.4" y="2.6" width="1.3" height="1.3"/><rect x="6.8" y="4.3" width="1.3" height="1.3"/><rect x="8.4" y="4.3" width="1.3" height="1.3"/><rect x="6.8" y="6" width="1.3" height="1.3"/><rect x="8.4" y="6" width="1.3" height="1.3"/>',
  subtract: '<circle cx="5" cy="5" r="4.2"/><path d="M2.6 5 H7.4"/>',
  subOnes: '<circle cx="3.4" cy="5" r="2.9"/><path d="M1.9 5 H4.9"/><rect x="7" y="3.6" width="2.6" height="2.6"/>',
  subTens: '<circle cx="3.4" cy="5" r="2.9"/><path d="M1.9 5 H4.9"/><rect x="7.4" y="0.6" width="2" height="8.8"/><path d="M7.4 2.4 H9.4 M7.4 4.1 H9.4 M7.4 5.8 H9.4 M7.4 7.5 H9.4"/>',
  ends: '<path d="M0.8 6.4 H9.2"/><path d="M0.8 3.6 V9.2 M9.2 3.6 V9.2"/><path d="M3.1 5.4 V7.4 M5 5.4 V7.4 M6.9 5.4 V7.4"/><circle cx="0.8" cy="1.5" r="1" class="f"/><circle cx="9.2" cy="1.5" r="1" class="f"/>',
  decide: '<path d="M3 9.2 V1.2"/><path d="M1.4 2.9 L3 1 L4.6 2.9"/><path d="M7 0.8 V8.8"/><path d="M5.4 7.1 L7 9 L8.6 7.1"/>',
  check: '<rect x="0.9" y="0.9" width="8.2" height="8.2" rx="1"/><path d="M2.6 5.2 L4.4 7 L7.6 2.9"/>',
  say: '<path d="M1.5 1.4 H8.5 Q9.3 1.4 9.3 2.2 V6 Q9.3 6.8 8.5 6.8 H4.4 L2.2 9 L2.6 6.8 H1.5 Q0.7 6.8 0.7 6 V2.2 Q0.7 1.4 1.5 1.4 Z"/>',
  // V21 additions for the word-problem routine (RCDWS) - same style:
  read: '<path d="M5 2.2 Q3 1 0.8 1.4 V8.4 Q3 8 5 9.2 Q7 8 9.2 8.4 V1.4 Q7 1 5 2.2 Z"/><path d="M5 2.2 V9.2"/>',
  ringnum: '<ellipse cx="5" cy="4.2" rx="4.1" ry="3.4"/><path d="M4.4 2.6 L5.4 2 V6.4"/><path d="M0.8 9.3 H9.2"/>',
  underline: '<path d="M0.8 5 Q2.4 1.6 4 5 Q5.6 8.4 7.2 5 Q8.2 3 9.2 4"/><path d="M0.8 9 H9.2"/>',
  draw: '<rect x="0.8" y="0.8" width="8.4" height="8.4" rx="1"/><path d="M2.4 6.8 Q3.6 3 5 5.4 Q6.4 7.6 7.6 3.2"/>',
  // V21 teacher icons (T-ICON), drawn in BLACK on teacher pages:
  tip: '<path d="M3.2 7.2 Q1.2 5.6 1.6 3.6 Q2.2 0.8 5 0.8 Q7.8 0.8 8.4 3.6 Q8.8 5.6 6.8 7.2 Z"/><path d="M3.4 8.4 H6.6 M3.8 9.6 H6.2"/>',
  feedback: '<path d="M1.5 1.4 H8.5 Q9.3 1.4 9.3 2.2 V6 Q9.3 6.8 8.5 6.8 H4.4 L2.2 9 L2.6 6.8 H1.5 Q0.7 6.8 0.7 6 V2.2 Q0.7 1.4 1.5 1.4 Z"/><path d="M3 4.2 L4.4 5.4 L7 2.8"/>',
  watch: '<path d="M0.8 5.6 Q5 1.2 9.2 5.6 Q5 10 0.8 5.6 Z"/><circle cx="5" cy="5.6" r="1.5" class="f"/><path d="M5 0.4 V2"/>',
  research: '<rect x="1.4" y="0.8" width="7.2" height="8.4" rx="0.6"/><path d="M3 0.8 V9.2"/><path d="M4.4 3 H7.2 M4.4 4.6 H7.2"/>',
  key: '<circle cx="3" cy="5" r="2.2"/><path d="M5.2 5 H9.4 M8 5 V7 M9.4 5 V6.6"/>',
  language: '<path d="M1.5 1.4 H8.5 Q9.3 1.4 9.3 2.2 V6 Q9.3 6.8 8.5 6.8 H4.4 L2.2 9 L2.6 6.8 H1.5 Q0.7 6.8 0.7 6 V2.2 Q0.7 1.4 1.5 1.4 Z"/><path d="M2.4 5.6 L3.6 2.4 L4.8 5.6 M2.9 4.4 H4.3"/><circle cx="6.6" cy="4.6" r="1"/><path d="M7.6 3.6 V5.6"/>',
  why: '<path d="M3.2 7.2 Q1.2 5.6 1.6 3.6 Q2.2 0.8 5 0.8 Q7.8 0.8 8.4 3.6 Q8.8 5.6 6.8 7.2 Z"/><path d="M3.4 8.4 H6.6"/><path d="M5 3 V5.4"/>',
  cubes: '<rect x="0.8" y="4.6" width="4" height="4"/><rect x="4.8" y="4.6" width="4" height="4"/><rect x="2.8" y="0.6" width="4" height="4"/>',
  board: '<rect x="0.8" y="0.8" width="8.4" height="5.6"/><path d="M2.4 6.4 L1.6 9.4 M7.6 6.4 L8.4 9.4"/>',
  mwb: '<rect x="1.6" y="2" width="6.8" height="6" rx="0.8"/><path d="M3 4 H6.4 M3 5.8 H5.4"/>',
  scissors: '<circle cx="2.2" cy="7.6" r="1.5"/><circle cx="2.2" cy="2.4" r="1.5"/><path d="M3.4 6.8 L9.4 1.8 M3.4 3.2 L9.4 8.2"/>',
};
// The icon at 5.8 mm (L). color 'accent' on pupil steps and teacher step rows; 'ink' for teacher box icons.
export function icon(id, { mm = 5.8, color = 'accent' } = {}) {
  const col = color === 'accent' ? 'var(--accent)' : '#000';
  return `<svg class="ico" ${color === 'accent' ? 'data-accent ' : ''}viewBox="0 0 10 10" width="${mm}mm" height="${mm}mm" style="color:${col}" aria-hidden="true">` +
    `<g fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="${r2(1.5 * PT / (mm / 10))}" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round">${ICONS[id]}</g></svg>`;
}

// VA-70. Vertical fact (one-digit + one-digit): HTML. state: 'blank' | 'traced' (answer grey) | key (answer in key ink).
// ring: 'top' | 'bottom' | null circles the big addend (black, or grey with ringGrey). tile: {at:'top'|'bottom', n, grey}.
export function factStack({ top, bottom, op = '+', ans = null, state = 'key', ring = null, ringGrey = false, tile = null } = {}) {
  const tileHTML = (at) => tile && tile.at === at ? `<span class="tile">${dotTile({ n: tile.n, grey: tile.grey })}</span>` : '';
  const ringHTML = (at) => ring === at ? `<i class="ring${ringGrey ? ' g' : ''}"></i>` : '';
  // digits sit right-aligned in tracks 2-3 (track 1 is the operator track); extras on the last digit only
  const row = (v, first, extra = '') => { const d = String(v).padStart(2, ' ').split('');
    return `${first}<span>${d[0].trim()}</span><span>${d[1]}${extra}</span>`; };
  const ad = ans == null ? ['', ''] : String(ans).padStart(2, ' ').split('');
  // state: 'blank' (empty answer row) | 'traced' (answer grey: newest model mark) | 'ink' (answer black: a finished example) | 'key' (answer shown only on keys)
  const wrap = (x) => (!x.trim() ? '' : state === 'traced' ? `<span class="g">${x}</span>` : state === 'ink' ? `<span>${x}</span>` : `<span class="ka">${x}</span>`);
  return `<div class="stk" data-slot="ans">` + row(top, '<span></span>', ringHTML('top') + tileHTML('top')) +
    row(bottom, `<span class="op">${op}</span>`, ringHTML('bottom') + tileHTML('bottom')) +
    `<i class="rule"></i><div class="ans"><span></span><span>${wrap(ad[0])}</span><span>${wrap(ad[1])}</span></div></div>`;
}

// Column stack with a regroup strip (VA-10, SL-12) for 2-digit addition: tracks 0.95 em, heads T O, regroup strip 8 mm,
// answer strip 12 mm tall, one segment per track. cells: answers on the key only.
// state: 'key' (marks only on the key) | 'grey' (model: carry and answer traced) | 'slot' (empty writing places)
export function columnStack({ top = 47, bottom = 25, op = '+', carry = '1', ans = '72', heads = true, key = false, state = key ? 'key' : 'slot' } = {}) {
  const t = String(top).padStart(2, ' '), b = String(bottom).padStart(2, ' '), A = String(ans).padStart(2, ' ');
  const seg = (k, n, v) => `<i style="display:block;height:${n === 'r' ? 8 : 12}mm;border:.75pt solid #000;${n === 'r' ? 'border-radius:1.5mm;' : k === 0 ? 'border-right-width:0;border-radius:1.5mm 0 0 1.5mm;' : 'border-radius:0 1.5mm 1.5mm 0;'}` +
    `font-style:normal;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:${n === 'r' ? 15 : 28}pt;${state === 'grey' ? 'border-color:#949494;border-width:1pt;' : ''}">` +
    `${!v.trim() ? '' : state === 'key' ? `<b class="ka">${v}</b>` : state === 'grey' ? `<span class="g">${v}</span>` : ''}</i>`;
  return `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,.95em);font-size:28pt;line-height:1;justify-content:center">` +
    (heads ? `<span></span><b style="font-size:12pt;text-align:center">T</b><b style="font-size:12pt;text-align:center">O</b>` : '') +
    `<span></span>${seg(0, 'r', carry)}<span style="border:0"></span>` +
    `<span></span><span style="text-align:center;height:1.15em">${t[0]}</span><span style="text-align:center">${t[1]}</span>` +
    `<b style="text-align:center">${op}</b><span style="text-align:center;height:1.15em">${b[0]}</span><span style="text-align:center">${b[1]}</span>` +
    `<i style="grid-column:1/-1;border-top:1.5pt solid #000;margin:1mm 0"></i>` +
    `<span></span>${seg(0, 'a', A[0])}${seg(1, 'a', A[1])}</div>`;
}

// VA-10 / VA-20 / SL-12. Two-digit column subtraction with a regroup strip (HTML, 28 pt, tracks 0.95 em: TY-21).
//   operator track | T | O.  Heads "T O" 12 pt bold over a 0.75 pt cap.  Regroup strip 8 mm over T and O
//   (one rounded outline, a divider on the track boundary; new tens left, new ones right, 15 pt).
//   Cross-out: a 0.75 pt diagonal through each regrouped digit.  Sum rule 1.5 pt.  Answer strip 12 mm, one
//   segment per track.  Optional Check line: "Check: [line] + bottom = [line]".
// Every mark takes a state: 'none' (absent), 'slot' (empty writing place), 'grey' (trace / newest model mark),
// 'ink' (black, done), 'key' (answer: shown only on the key, in the key colour).  A tens answer of 0 is left empty.
export function columnStackSub({ top, bottom, op = '−', regroup = 'slot', cross = 'none', ans = 'slot', ansDigits = null,
  check = false, checkState = 'slot', heads = true, size = 28, wide = false, ring = null } = {}) {
  // ring: 'ones' draws a grey 1 pt ellipse round both ones digits (the 'Look at the ones' mark)
  const t = String(top).padStart(2, ' '), b = String(bottom).padStart(2, ' ');
  const diff = top - bottom, d = ansDigits || [Math.floor(diff / 10) || '', diff % 10];
  const regT = Math.floor(top / 10) - 1, regO = (top % 10) + 10;
  const mark = (v, st) => st === 'grey' ? `<span class="g">${v}</span>` : st === 'ink' ? `<span>${v}</span>` : st === 'key' ? `<b class="ka">${v}</b>` : '';
  const grey = (st) => st === 'grey' ? 'border-color:#949494;border-width:1pt;' : '';
  const seg = (pos, h, inner, st) => `<i data-slot="${st === 'none' ? '' : 'strip'}" style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-style:normal;height:${h}mm;` +
    `border:.75pt solid #000;${pos === 'L' ? 'border-right-width:0;border-radius:1.5mm 0 0 1.5mm;' : 'border-radius:0 1.5mm 1.5mm 0;'}${grey(st)}">${inner}</i>`;
  const xo = (st) => st === 'none' ? '' : `<svg viewBox="0 0 10 10" preserveAspectRatio="none" style="position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;overflow:visible"${st === 'key' ? ' class="ka" data-ka' : ''}>` +
    `<path d="M7.6 1.2 L2.6 9.2" stroke="${st === 'grey' ? '#949494' : '#000'}" stroke-width="${st === 'grey' ? '1pt' : '.75pt'}" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"${st === 'key' ? ' data-ka' : ''}/></svg>`;
  const regroupRow = regroup === 'none' ? `<span style="grid-column:1/-1;height:8mm"></span>` :
    `<span></span>${seg('L', 8, `<span style="font-size:15pt">${mark(regT, regroup)}</span>`, regroup)}${seg('R', 8, `<span style="font-size:15pt">${mark(regO, regroup)}</span>`, regroup)}`;
  const rg = (on) => on ? `<i style="position:absolute;left:50%;top:50%;width:.8em;height:1.1em;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);border:1pt solid #949494;border-radius:50%"></i>` : '';
  const dig = (v, st, r = false) => `<span style="position:relative;display:flex;justify-content:center;height:1.15em;align-items:center">${v.trim()}${v.trim() ? xo(st) : ''}${rg(r && v.trim())}</span>`;
  const ansRow = ans === 'none' ? `<span style="grid-column:1/-1;height:12mm"></span>` :
    `<span></span>${seg('L', 12, mark(d[0], d[0] === '' ? 'none' : ans), ans)}${seg('R', 12, mark(d[1], ans), ans)}`;
  const chk = check ? `<div class="ws-checkrow" style="display:flex;align-items:flex-end;justify-content:center;gap:1.6mm;margin-top:2mm;font-size:15pt;white-space:nowrap">` +
    `<b>Check:</b><span class="ln" data-slot="line" style="--w:17mm;height:7mm;line-height:7mm">${mark(diff, checkState)}</span><span>+ ${bottom} =</span>` +
    `<span class="ln" data-slot="line" style="--w:17mm;height:7mm;line-height:7mm">${mark(top, checkState)}</span></div>` : '';
  return `<div class="stk2" data-slot="ans" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center"><div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,${wide ? 1.1 : 0.95}em);font-size:${size}pt;line-height:1">` +
    (heads ? `<span></span><b style="font-size:12pt;text-align:center;height:6mm">T</b><b style="font-size:12pt;text-align:center;height:6mm">O</b><i style="grid-column:2/-1;border-top:.75pt solid #000;height:1mm"></i>` : '') +
    regroupRow + `<span></span>${dig(t[0], cross)}${dig(t[1], cross, ring === 'ones')}` +
    `<b style="text-align:center;height:1.15em;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center">${op}</b>${dig(b[0], 'none')}${dig(b[1], 'none', ring === 'ones')}` +
    `<i style="grid-column:1/-1;border-top:1.5pt solid #000;margin:1mm 0 1.5mm"></i>` + ansRow + `</div>${chk}</div>`;
}

// RP-120 / RP-93. A 2D shape to judge, name, partition or shade (bounding box 42 mm at L).
//   kind: rect | square | circle | triangle.  Outline 1.5 pt for a shape to judge or name, 0.75 pt for one the pupil
//   partitions or shades; no fill.
//   lines: [{x1, y1, x2, y2, state}] partition lines in FRACTIONS of the box (0..1): state 'ink' (given, 0.75 pt),
//          'grey' (model, 1 pt grey), 'key' (the answer: key only). An unequal partition is just an off-centre line.
//   shade: [[x, y], ...] a polygon in fractions of the box, filled flat grey (#949494): the given shaded part.
//   guides: [[x, y], ...] guide dots (1.2 mm, grey) where a line may start and end. A HINT scaffold: Guided cells only,
//          and placed on every candidate point (all 4 side midpoints of a rectangle), never only on the answer's ends.
let shapeIds = 0;
export function shape({ kind = 'rect', w = 42, h = 30, task = 'judge', lines = [], shade = null, guides = [] } = {}) {
  const sw = r2(task === 'judge' ? S.heavy : S.hair), p = 1;
  if (kind === 'square' || kind === 'circle') h = w;
  const X = (f) => r2(p + f * w), Y = (f) => r2(p + f * h);
  let out = '', clip = '';
  if (kind === 'circle') clip = `<circle cx="${X(0.5)}" cy="${Y(0.5)}" r="${r2(w / 2)}"/>`;
  else if (kind === 'triangle') clip = `<path d="M${X(0.5)} ${Y(0)} L${X(1)} ${Y(1)} L${X(0)} ${Y(1)} Z"/>`;
  else clip = `<rect x="${p}" y="${p}" width="${w}" height="${h}"/>`;
  const id = 'sh' + (++shapeIds);
  if (shade) out += `<clipPath id="${id}">${clip}</clipPath><path clip-path="url(#${id})" d="M${shade.map(([x, y]) => `${X(x)} ${Y(y)}`).join(' L')} Z" fill="${GREY}" stroke="none"/>`;
  out += clip.replace('/>', ` stroke-width="${sw}"/>`);
  for (const l of lines) out += `<path d="M${X(l.x1)} ${Y(l.y1)} L${X(l.x2)} ${Y(l.y2)}" stroke="${l.state === 'grey' ? GREY : '#000'}" stroke-width="${r2(l.state === 'grey' ? S.dot : S.hair)}"${l.state === 'key' ? ' data-ka' : ''}/>`;
  for (const [x, y] of guides) out += `<circle cx="${X(x)}" cy="${Y(y)}" r="0.6" fill="${GREY}" stroke="none"/>`;
  return svg(w + 2 * p, h + 2 * p, out, 'v-shape');
}
```

### D.2 Pack kit (copy whole): the page frame, teacher parts and pack assembly

`makeKit(L, R)` returns the frame builders (header with the lift rule, footer with the © line, strand tab,
`pupilPage`, the 2-up `exitPage`), the teacher parts (`sec`, `tbox`, `sc`, `loop`, `table`, `withThumb`), and
`document()`, which orders the pack: teacher pages, the pupil packet, one facsimile key per pupil page (the
anchor chart included), then T6. `poolCheck()` reports exact repeats and same-page turn-arounds (E24).

```js v21-packkit
// ===== V21 pack kit (copy whole): the page frame, teacher-page parts and pack assembly. =====
// Pure functions over a lesson description L and the drawing recipes R (Appendix D). Every page a lesson
// builds goes through `page()` so the frame (header, strand tab, title, footer, © line) is identical everywhere.
// L = { title, level, strand, foot, seed, steps: [[iconId, text], ...] }
export function makeKit(L, R) {
  const { icon } = R;
  const LET = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
  const COPY = `© ${L.year || 2026} Cultivating the Digital. All rights reserved.`;
  const tab = (l3, two = false) => `<div class="tab"><span>${L.level}</span>${two ? '' : `<span>${L.strand}</span>`}<span>${l3}</span></div>`;
  // Full header (fields) or lifted header (no fields: anchor chart, teacher pages).
  function header({ fields = true, score = null, l3, key = false }) {
    // Lift the title into row A only when it is <= 118 mm wide (about 34 characters at 18 pt, HD reflow); else row A holds the tab alone.
    if (!fields && L.title.length <= 34) return `<div class="hd"><div class="rowA"><div class="title">${L.title}</div>${tab(key ? 'Answer Key' : l3)}</div><div class="hrule"></div></div>`;
    if (!fields) return `<div class="hd"><div class="rowA">${tab(key ? 'Answer Key' : l3)}</div><div class="title">${L.title}</div><div class="hrule"></div></div>`;
    return `<div class="hd"><div class="rowA">` +
      `<div class="fld name">Name <i>${key ? 'Answer Key' : ''}</i></div><div class="fld date">Date <i></i></div>` +
      (score != null ? `<div class="fld score">Score <i></i><b>/${score}</b></div>` : '') + tab(key ? 'Answer Key' : l3) +
      `</div><div class="title">${L.title}</div><div class="hrule"></div></div>`;
  }
  const footer = (centre, right) => `<div class="ft"><span>${L.foot}</span><span><b>${centre}</b></span><span>${right}</span><span class="copy">${COPY}</span></div>`;
  const page = ({ id, cls = '', head, body, foot }) => `<section class="pg ${cls}" data-page="${id}">${head}<div class="body">${body}</div>${foot}</section>`;
  const stepIcons = (i) => `<span class="num" data-accent>${i + 1}</span>${icon(L.steps[i][0])}`;
  const stepsStrip = () => `<div class="sstrip"><b>Steps:</b><ol>${L.steps.map(([, t], i) => `<li>${stepIcons(i)}<span>${t}</span></li>`).join('')}</ol></div>`;
  const stepsList = () => `<ol class="steps" style="flex:1;padding:2mm 3mm 3mm">${L.steps.map(([, t], i) => `<li>${stepIcons(i)}<span>${t}</span></li>`).join('')}</ol>`;
  // every dealt item carries data-item="a+b" (or a−b) and its grid row, so the self-check reads items from the page
  const it = (a, b, op, row = 0, copy = false) => copy ? '' : ` data-item="${a}${op}${b}" data-row="${row}"`;
  const k = (v) => `<b class="ka">${v}</b>`;                     // an answer: shown only on the key
  const lt = (i) => `<span class="lt">${LET[i]}.</span>`;
  // ---- teacher parts ----
  const sec = (title, inner, grow = false) => `<div class="band${grow ? ' grow' : ''}"><div class="strip"><b>${title}</b></div><div class="tpad" style="border-top:1.5pt solid #000;${grow ? 'flex:1;' : ''}">${inner}</div></div>`;
  const tbox = (kind, lab, title, text) => `<div class="tbox${kind === 'watch' ? ' watch' : ''}">${icon(kind, { color: 'ink' })}<div><b>${lab}</b> <b>${title}</b> ${text}</div></div>`;
  const sc = (n, rows) => `<div class="sc"><span class="num" data-accent>${n}</span>${rows.map(([l, t]) => `<b>${l}</b><span${/^(Say|Ask)/.test(l) ? ' class="q"' : ''}>${t}</span>`).join('')}</div>`;
  const ans = (t) => `<span class="ka">${t}</span>`;               // expected answer on a teacher page
  const blank = (mm = 17) => `<span class="ln" style="--w:${mm}mm;height:5mm"></span>`;  // a said blank, never underscores
  const LOOP = { most: '<circle cx="2.5" cy="2.5" r="2" fill="#000"/>', half: '<circle cx="2.5" cy="2.5" r="2" fill="none" stroke="#000" stroke-width=".27"/><path d="M2.5 .5 A2 2 0 0 0 2.5 4.5 Z" fill="#000"/>', few: '<circle cx="2.5" cy="2.5" r="2" fill="none" stroke="#000" stroke-width=".27"/>' };
  const loop = (rows) => `<div class="loop">${rows.map(([kk, lab, t]) => `<svg viewBox="0 0 5 5" width="4mm" height="4mm">${LOOP[kk]}</svg><b>${lab}</b><span>${t}</span>`).join('')}</div>`;
  const loopIcon = (kk) => `<svg viewBox="0 0 5 5" width="4mm" height="4mm" style="display:inline-block;vertical-align:-.6mm">${LOOP[kk]}</svg>`;
  const num = (c) => /^(<b>)?\d+(<\/b>)?$/.test(String(c));
  const table = (heads, rows) => `<table class="tt"><tr>${heads.map((h, i) => `<th${i === heads.length - 1 && /^(min|fade)$/i.test(h) ? ' class="r"' : ''}>${h}</th>`).join('')}</tr>` +
    rows.map((r) => `<tr>${r.map((c, i) => `<td${i === r.length - 1 && (num(c) || /^★+$/.test(String(c))) ? ' class="r"' : ''}>${c}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('') + `</table>`;
  const chk = (t) => `<div><span class="chk" style="width:5mm;height:5mm;vertical-align:-1mm"></span>${t}</div>`;
  const thumb = (pageHtml) => `<div class="thumb">${pageHtml}</div>`;
  // A section with a pupil-page thumbnail at the right of its first rows (§10.9).
  const withThumb = (left, pageHtml) => `<div style="display:flex;gap:4mm"><div style="flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:1.5mm;min-width:0">${left}</div>${thumb(pageHtml)}</div>`;
  const tpage = (id, l3, body, n, N, right = 'Teacher · Form A · seed ' + L.seed, key = false) =>
    page({ id, cls: key ? 'key teacher' : 'teacher', head: header({ fields: false, l3 }), body: `<div class="t" style="display:contents">${body}</div>`, foot: footer(`${n}/${N}`, right) });
  // ---- pupil page wrappers ----
  const pupilPage = ({ id, l3, score, body, key = false, fields = true }) => page({ id: id + (key ? '-key' : ''), cls: key ? 'key' : 'pupil',
    head: header({ fields, score, l3, key }), body, foot: footer('1/1', `${key ? 'Answer Key · ' : ''}Form A · seed ${L.seed}`) });
  // 2-up exit slip: A4 landscape, two identical 132.5 mm halves, 6 mm between them with the dashed cut line (§9.7).
  const exitPage = ({ id = 'P6', half, key = false }) => `<section class="pg land ${key ? 'key' : 'pupil'}" data-page="${id}${key ? '-key' : ''}">` +
    `<div class="body"><div style="flex:1;display:flex;gap:6mm;position:relative">${half(key, 0)}<div class="cut" data-cut></div>` +
    `<div style="position:absolute;left:50%;top:0;transform:translate(-50%,0);background:#fff;padding:1mm 0">${icon('scissors', { color: 'ink', mm: 5.8 })}</div>${half(key, 1)}</div></div>` +
    footer('1/1', `${key ? 'Answer Key · ' : ''}Form A · seed ${L.seed}`) + `</section>`;
  const exitHead = (key) => `<div class="rowA" style="gap:3mm"><div class="fld name" style="min-width:40mm">Name <i>${key ? 'Answer Key' : ''}</i></div>` +
    `<div class="fld score">Score <i></i><b>/4</b></div>${tab(key ? 'Answer Key' : 'Exit slip')}</div><div class="title">${L.title}</div><div class="hrule"></div>`;
  const exitFoot = () => `<div style="flex:none;display:flex;gap:10mm;padding:3mm 3mm 0;font-size:15pt"><div><span class="chk" data-slot="check"></span>I can do this.</div><div><span class="chk" data-slot="check"></span>I need one more day.</div></div>`;
  // ---- assembly: teacher pages, the pupil packet, then keys of every pupil page (the anchor chart too), then T6 ----
  // pool: { op, pool: [[a, b], ...], forbidden: [[a, b], ...], edge: [[a, b], ...] } is written INTO the pack as JSON
  // (<script type="application/json" id="v21-pool">) so the self-check (E24, E24b) needs nothing but pack.html.
  function document({ teacher, pupil, routing, titleTag, pool }) {
    const N = teacher.length + 1;
    const pages = teacher.map((t, i) => tpage(t.id, t.l3, t.body, i + 1, N, t.right));
    for (const p of pupil) pages.push(p.render(false));
    for (const p of pupil) pages.push(p.render(true));
    pages.push(tpage('T6', 'Answer Key', routing, N, N, undefined, true));
    return { pages, html: (css) => `<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">` +
      `<title>${titleTag}</title><style>${css}</style>` + (pool ? `<script type="application/json" id="v21-pool">${JSON.stringify(pool)}</script>` : '') +
      `</head><body>${pages.join('\n')}</body></html>` };
  }
  return { it, LET, tab, header, footer, page, stepsStrip, stepsList, stepIcons, k, lt, sec, tbox, sc, ans, blank, loop, loopIcon, table, chk, thumb, withThumb,
    tpage, pupilPage, exitPage, exitHead, exitFoot, document };
}
```

---

## Appendix E — Pre-output self-check (MANDATORY; every check must pass before the pack is issued)

Render the HTML in a browser with print media, A4, after `document.fonts.ready`, and measure. Units: 1 mm
= 3.7795 CSS px; 1 pt = 1.3333 CSS px. "Ink" = the client rectangles of text, SVGs, slots (`.bx .ln .sq
.circ .chk`), sum rules, rings, dots, number tracks and story boxes; quiet letters are excluded from band
measurements; the 3 mm cell padding is structure, not an empty band.

| # | Check | How to measure | Pass |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | Paper | PDF page size | every page 595 × 842 pt (A4) or 842 × 595 pt (the 2-up slip); Letter only when asked |
| E1b | Half-page geometry | width of each exit-slip half | 132.5 ± 0.5 mm |
| E2 | Andika | `document.fonts.check('700 28px Andika')`, `('400 15px Andika')`; computed `font-family` of every text node | both true; 0 text nodes in another face |
| E3 | Paints | computed `color` (text), `background-color`, border colours (width > 0), SVG `fill` / `stroke` of every element | only #000, #FFF, #949494, plus #5B2A86 inside a step mark and #C2410C inside an answer mark |
| E4 | No colour on pupil pages | as E3, pupil pages | only #000, #FFF, #949494, #5B2A86 (step marks) |
| E5 | No emoji | every character tested against `\p{Extended_Pictographic}` (© and the teacher-page ★ excepted) | 0 |
| E6 | Pupil type floor | smallest computed size × SVG scale of pupil text, excluding quiet letters (10 pt), strand tab (10 pt), footer (7 / 6.5 pt) | ≥ 11 pt |
| E7 | Teacher type floor | as E6 on teacher pages | ≥ 10 pt |
| E8 | Working digits | computed size of every equation and stack outside the anchor chart | exactly 28 pt |
| E9 | Page used, nothing past the body | (lowest ink − body top) ÷ body height, per page | 50–100 % |
| E9b | Bottom blank (site lint) | 100 % − E9 | ≤ 8 % on every page |
| E10 | Free per cell | 1 mm raster of ink in each item cell (slots and answer rows are ink; reading cells exempt) | ≥ 40 % free |
| E11 | Empty bands, exactly as the site's `sheetCheck` | box round every leaf element (no child elements, an SVG, or a slot) and every bordered element, the item letter excepted; gaps measured from the cell's **border box** (padding counts as empty); cells under 40 px skipped; draw / work cells exempt | largest top/bottom gap < 30 % of the height and largest left/right gap < 30 % of the width |
